## Superseded — the W6 Next action, kept for the record

**The eleven item types render, and for the first time in this rebuild there is
something a human can look at.** W6 is code-complete and **not deployed**. Suite
**1340 passing / 6 skipped / 0 failing** (baseline 1275), plus **39 frontend
tests** — the first the frontend has ever had. **No migration: `schema_version`
stays at 12**, and there is no 013 to go looking for; 013 belongs to W7.

**The task row's criterion is not met as written, and was not adjusted.**
*"All 11 types render and grade correctly on a phone"* cannot be met while W14
(migration 018) is outside the fence. The measured claim is: **nine of eleven
render and grade correctly; `speak_repeat` and `speak_answer` render and are
self-marked** under `graded_by='self'`, which is an instrument and not a score.
The split was put to the human and **ruled on 2026-08-25** — reporting the number
rather than moving the bar (CLAUDE.md §3 rule 7).

**Three rulings were given on 2026-08-25** and are in the decisions log as
rulings given that day: audio delivery (`GET /items/{id}/audio`), the spoken
types self-marked, and #80 fixed at build time. An earlier draft of the plan had
recorded all three as already ruled when they had not been; that correction is
also in the log, because a record carrying an authority nobody gave is worse than
one carrying a call made unilaterally.

### Deploy, then check — in this order

Nothing below has been run. Every step is the human's; Claude Code has no SSH
access to this host and is not to be given any. The host is shared with
`fonderis-worker`: no broad upgrades, no reboot, no Caddy rewrite.

1. **Backup.** `bash scripts/backup.sh` → a new dated dump and its R2 key.
2. **Pull** to the W6 commit; `git log --oneline -1`.
3. **`pip install -e packages/core`** → `Successfully installed core-…`.
4. **`pytest -q`** → no fewer than 1275 passing, zero failing. Record the total.
5. **`cd apps/web && pnpm install && pnpm test`** → the Vitest pass count, with
   eleven render tests among them. **A suite that exists and is never run is the
   trap #67 was opened for.**
6. **`python -m core.db status`** → **exactly** `Applied: 001–012, Pending:
   (none)`. A pending 013 means W6 wrote a migration it was told not to write —
   stop.
7. **Restart `english-api` only.** Log line `API built origins=… routes=…` naming
   the `/items` paths.
8. **`curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' https://api.foundgrant.com/items`**
   → **401**. The route exists and refuses an anonymous caller.
9. **Vercel:** `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL` must be set **before** redeploying — with
   #80 fixed the production build now **fails** on a missing or empty value
   rather than shipping a broken bundle. That is the point; it is here so nobody
   is surprised by it at 2am.
10. **Identify a non-learner `users.id`.** `psql` directly — `python -m core.db`
    takes only `migrate` and `status`, and `.env` must not be sourced (#98). Run
    `SELECT current_database()` first: PostgreSQL on this host serves
    `fonderis-worker` too. Both queries are read-only.
11. **Seed, once, by hand.** `python -m core.items.seed_fixtures --user <ID>`
    then `--live`. Billed: eleven validations including three audio round-trips.
    **Paste the accept-rate table into the decisions log** — it is the first real
    accept-rate datum for the W5a probe, which the W10 row says nobody has.
12. **The human checks, on a phone** (below).
13. **Purge.** `--purge`, then confirm the row count. **#109 tracks this.** Left
    undone, W10's first `assign_daily` can build a real session out of test
    fixtures — including an `l1_to_l2_production` whose canonical is trusted, not
    verified (#102).

### W6's human checks — what each catches that no test can

1. **All eleven opened on a real phone, answered, feedback read.** Catches a
   projection that is complete and practically unanswerable — a word bank that
   does not fit the width, a `match_pairs` with no visible way to pair, an
   `error_spot` whose tiles wrap mid-sentence. No test can look at a layout and
   say a person could not do this.
   **What this check will NOT show:** no fixture carries an `explanation` and
   none declares an `error_type`, so the panel renders **the cue and the
   canonical answer for every item**. The record must say *the fallback* was
   verified, never *the panel* was (#103, #104).
2. **A typed answer with wrong capitalisation and a trailing full stop, on the
   phone's own keyboard with autocorrect live.** Catches the one thing Vitest
   cannot — jsdom has no autocorrect. `autoCorrect="off"` is a *request* to iOS
   Safari, and whether it is honoured for a given input is an empirical question
   about a real device. **This is the only place the autocorrect question can be
   settled.**
3. **One-handed tap reachability, and no layout shift when the keyboard opens.**
   Catches a submit button under the thumb-shadow or below the keyboard, and the
   iOS focus-zoom that a computed-style assertion approximates but cannot
   reproduce.
4. **The wrong-answer path read for guilt copy, by a person, in the app.**
   Catches what the regex cannot — tone. "That's not it" passes every banned
   pattern and still reads as a rebuke. A scan finds only words someone already
   thought to ban.
5. **`dictation` and `listening_gap` played with the phone on silent, and on
   mobile data.** Catches an audio element that plays in desktop Chrome and is
   blocked by iOS autoplay policy or the hardware mute switch, and a synthesis
   latency that is fine on wifi and not on 4G.
6. **The accept-rate table read as a number.** Catches nothing — that is the
   point. It is the first real datum for W10, and it licenses **no claim about
   the gate's rate**: eleven items, one sample each, of a stochastic system.
7. **The purge confirmed** (step 13). Catches the failure that would otherwise
   surface at W10 as a learner being taught from a test fixture.

### Still unrun, carried forward — every one, named and not silently

**W6 clears none of these.** It is the first slice to exercise a learner path at
all, and the path it exercises is its own.

1. **The hand-checked coverage number. Unrun since W4, and still the one that
   matters.** Take a paragraph of English the human has actually read, run it
   through `coverage_for`, and judge whether the percentage matches how hard it
   felt. **No test can substitute for this.** W12 selects video on this number.
2. **The 20-item Life sample — and it still cannot be run today.** It is W5's own
   check and it needs *generated* items. The eleven W6 seeds are committed
   fixtures, not generator output, so they do not answer "do these sound like a
   person talking to a friend?" **It belongs to the first `assign_daily` run at
   W10.**
3. **`/stats` in Telegram on the restructured bot.** W1's, and the last of that

## Superseded — the W5a Next action, kept for the record

**Items have a schema and a validator, and the uniqueness gate works.** Migration
012 is live (`Applied: 001–012, Pending: (none)`); W5a is deployed on top of it
with **no migration of its own** — `schema_version` stays at **12**, and a later
reader should not go looking for an 013 that never existed. The gate detects
multi-acceptability and is **verified live in both directions, exit 0**: the item
that started the v3 rebuild is now caught and repaired by the gate built for it.
Suite **1275 passing / 0 failing**.

**Nothing has been generated.** `items` is empty by design and stays empty until
W10's `assign_daily`. No learner sees anything different after either deploy — if
one does, something is wrong.

**What the live pass licenses, unchanged by `exit 0`:** one sample per item of a
stochastic system, no claim about the gate's rate, a fail decisive and a pass not
proof — and a model resolves cues a B1 learner will miss, so a clean probe is
weaker evidence about a learner than it looks. The first run of that same command
produced a false alarm, which is precisely why the caveat stays attached.

4. **The four Telegram explanation paths — `/diary`, a voice message, `/capture`,
